- simplex_project(v): exact. Implement using Duchi:
```
def simplex_project(v):
    x = np.asarray(v)
    u = np.sort(x)[::-1]
    sv = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1, len(x)+1) > (sv - 1))[0][0]
    theta = (sv[rho] - 1) / (rho + 1.0)
    return np.maximum(x - theta, 0.0)
```
This projects onto sum=1, nonneg. Need if x all equal? Works. Use n-1? sv length n. Condition u * np.arange(1,n+1) > (sv - 1). For n maybe large, okay. If no nonzero? fallback uniform. Ensure sum maybe 1.
- normalize_simplex(a): np.maximum(a,0); if sum<1e-12 uniform; else a/sum.

- convolve_fft(a): M = next_pow2(2*n-1); fa = rfft(a,M); c = irfft(fa*fa, M)[:2*n-1]; clip.
- corr_fft(w,a): L=len(w), n=len(a); M=next_pow2(L+n-1); return irfft(rfft(a,M)*rfft(w,M).conj(), M)[:n].

- get_gradient_direction(a, p, T maybe): compute c, m=max(c); if m<=0 return zeros.

   if p==1: w=np.full(L,1.0)

Adam on simplex:
```
def adam_simplex(a, iters, deadline, p, lr_schedule, beta1,beta2,eps, target):
    n=len(a); a=a.copy(); normalize
    m_t=np.zeros(n); v_t=np.zeros(n)
    lr = initial_lr
    best=a.copy(); best_score=eval_exact(a)
    for it in range(iters):
       if time>deadline break
       c=convolve_fft(a); maxc=c.max(); score=2*n*maxc
       if score<best_score: best...
       if maxc==0: break
       ratio=c/maxc
       if p==1: w=ones/L
       else: w=ratio**(p-1)
       s=w.sum(); if s<=0: w uniform else w/=s
       h=corr_fft(w,a)
       grad = h  # raw direction to minimize? We need minimize Q=Σw c, gradient is h (up to factor). So update a - lr*grad.
       # Adam
       m_t = beta1*m_t + (1-beta1)*grad
       v_t = beta2*v_t + (1-beta2)*grad*grad
       m_hat=m_t/(1-beta1**(it+1)); v_hat=v_t/(1-beta2**(it+1))
       a_new = a - lr * m_hat/(sqrt(v_hat)+eps)
       a_new = simplex_project(a_new)
       # optional line search / rollback
       # evaluate new exact? We already have c for old, need new exact maybe score. To avoid extra FFT? We can compute next iter anyway. For rollback, compute score_new = 2n*max(convolve_fft(a_new)). This doubles conv cost. Maybe do every few iters or if needed. Simpler accept; Adam local may oscillate but we keep best.
       a = a_new
    return best,best_score
```

Let's design a spectral optimization loop:
```
def spectral_iteration(a, M_target, n_iters=100):
    a = normalize(a)
    for it:
       c=convolve_fft(a)
       cmax=c.max()
       if cmax < M_target: M_target= cmax*0.9
       c_t = smooth_clip(c, M_target)
       C=fft(c_t, fftlen)
       A=sqrt_complex(C)
       # enforce conjugate symmetry: A[:n//2+1] principal, mirror conj
       a_new=irfft(A, fftlen).real[:n]
       a_new=np.clip(a_new,0,None)
       if sum small: a_new=a
       a=normalize(a_new)
       maybe blend with old: a=0.7*a_new+0.3*a
    return a
```

Let's detail Adam function with K phases.

```
def adam_phases(a, max_iters, deadline, k_vals, lr0, print_prefix):
    n=len(a); a=normalize(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=exact_score(a)
    for K in k_vals:
       a=best.copy()
       m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
       lr=lr0 / (1+np.log1p(K))? maybe
       for it in range(max_iters):
          if time>deadline: break
          h, c, mC = gradient_weights(a,K)
          score=2*n*mC
          if score<best_score: best...
          if gradient norm zero: break
          m=...; v=...; update; project
          # if no improvement for 200, random perturbation
       # after phase, maybe best
    return best,best_score
```

Random perturbation: Need be top-level.
```
def random_perturb(a, rng, t):
    n=len(a)
    # choose anti-symmetric random noise
    r = rng.normal(0,1,n)
    r = r - r[::-1]  # anti-pal? r_j - r_{n-1-j}
    a2 = a + t*r
    a2 = np.maximum(a2,0)
    s=a2.sum(); if s<1e-12: return normalize(a)
    return a2/s
```
Mix with random simplex:
```
def mix_random(a, rng, t):
    b=rng.random(n)
    return normalize((1-t)*a + t*b)
```

LP function:
```
def lp_improve(a, deadline, max_solutions=3):
    n=len(a)
    if n>lp_max: return a,score
    a=normalize(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=exact_score(a)
    current=a.copy()
    for outer in range(max_solutions):
       if time>deadline: break
       M = convolve_fft(current).max()
       if M <=0: break
       A_ub = build_convolution_matrix(current)
       try:
           res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1, M), bounds=[(0,1000)]*n, method='highs')
       except Exception: break
       if res.success and res.x is not None:
           b=np.asarray(res.x)
           b=np.maximum(b,0); s=b.sum(); if s<=1+1e-8? continue
           b_norm=b/s
           b_norm=normalize(b_norm)
           score=exact_score(b_norm)
           if score<best_score:
               best=b_norm; best_score=score
               # maybe set current = mix(0.5 current + 0.5 b_norm) or current=b_norm
               # SOTA uses small t; let's try both: if b better, current=0.7*b+0.3*current? To avoid jumping? b itself feasible; accept b.
               current=b_norm
       else: break
       # optional reduce M? Already next current.
    return best,best_score
```

Clip target function:
```
def clip_convolution(c, M):
   c=np.asarray(c,float); L=len(c)
   cap = min(M, c.max()) maybe if M>cmax no clip
   c_t=np.minimum(c, M)
   s=c_t.sum()
   if s <=0: return c/c.max? 
   # Need sum 1? For spectral target, we can normalize to original sum (1)
   # If clipping reduced sum, scale all up, but capped entries exceed M. Instead distribute residual to uncapped.
   total_target = c.sum() # 1
   if total_target <=0: return c + eps
   if s >= total_target:
       return c_t / s  # just normalize
   # capacity: num_uncapped = (c_t < M).sum(); if enough add
   unc = c_t < M
   # We can add residual to unc uniformly without exceeding M:
   headroom = (M - c_t[unc]).sum()
   if headroom <=0:
       return c_t / s
   add = min(1.0, (total_target - s)/headroom)
   c_t2 = c_t.copy()
   c_t2[unc] += add*(M - c_t[unc])
   # After scaling? sum now total_target
   return c_t2
```
But if M too small and capacity M*L < total_target, impossible; choose M not too small. L≈2n, M=0.75/n capacity≈1.5, OK.

Function `compute_c_and_h(a, K)`:
```
def compute_c_and_h(a, K):
   n=len(a); L=2*n-1; M=fft_len(n)
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a, M)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, M)[:L]
   c=np.clip(c,0,None)
   m=c.max()
   if m<=1e-300: return c,m,None
   z=(c-m)/(m/K)  # K positive
   w=np.exp(z)
   s=w.sum()
   if s<=0: w=np.full(L,1.0/L)
   else: w/=s
   fw=np.fft.rfft(w, M)
   h=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw.conj(), M)[:n]
   return c,m,h
```
This computes weights sum1. If K very small, z near 0, w uniform. Good.

But note h with normalized w. The gradient direction scale ~1/n. Adam delta normalized. Good.

```
score=2*n*m
...
# Adam update using h
```

Candidate optimization function:
```
def optimize_candidate(a0, deadline, k_values, total_iters, rng):
   a=normalize(a0); n=len(a)
   best=a.copy(); best_score=2*n*convolveFFT(a).max()
   iters_per_phase = max(50, total_iters//len(k_values))
   for idx,K in enumerate(k_values):
       if time>=deadline: break
       a=best.copy()
       mom=np.zeros(n); momv=np.zeros(n)
       beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; eps=1e-8
       lr0=0.02/(1+0.3*np.log1p(K))
       no_improve=0
       for it in range(iters_per_phase):
           if time>=deadline: break
           c,m,h = compute_c_and_h(a,K)
           score = 2*n*m
           if score < best_score - 1e-12:
               best_score=score; best=a.copy(); no_improve=0
           else:
               no_improve += 1
           if no_improve > 500:
               # perturb best
               a=random_perturb(best, rng, 0.1)
               # maybe mix with random
               mom=np.zeros(n); momv=np.zeros(n)
               no_improve=0
           if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)): break
           mom=(1-? formula) ...
           step=mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+eps)
           a=project_simplex(a - lr*step)
       # after phase, if score worse, restart best
   return best,best_score
```


Implementation details:
```
def spectral_initialize(n, rng, iters=30):
    a = np.ones(n)/n
    # maybe random initial a = rng.random(n); normalize
    for _ in range(iters):
       c=convolution_fft(a); m=c.max()
       # target M ratio maybe 0.8/n, but if current m lower? Use target = min(m*0.95, 0.9/n)
       Mtarget = min(0.9/n, m*0.95)
       c_t = cap_convolution(c, Mtarget, total=1.0)
       L=len(c_t); M=next_pow2(L)
       C=np.fft.rfft(c_t, M)
       A=np.sqrt(C)
       # try candidates
       best_cand=None; best_s=inf
       for sign in [1,-1]:
          x=sign*np.fft.irfft(A, M)[:n]
          for take in ['clip','abs']:
             if take=='clip': y=np.clip(x,0,None)
             else: y=np.abs(x)
             y=normalize(y)
             sc=2*n*convolution_fft(y).max()
             if sc<best_s: best_s=sc; best_cand=y
       if best_cand is None: break
       # blend with old
       a=normalize(0.6*best_cand+0.4*a)
    return a
```
This does many convs per iter (3 candidates), iters 30 -> 90 convs; okay for few n. Maybe reduce iters 10, candidates fewer: only clip of positive and abs. Use shadow? Maybe not.

LP details more. Let's implement `build_convolution_matrix(a)` using sparse. To avoid large memory, if n > 1200, use dense? Sparse for 1200 1.44M. Fine. But constructing n^2 arrays can be memory. We can build with `diags` easier memory maybe. Let's derive `diags` construction:
We need A[k,j]=a[k-j] if 0<=k-j<n.
Let offsets d = 0..n-1 (k = j+d). For each d, a[d] on the d-th superdiagonal (above main) because row k = col j + d, so row-col = -d. In scipy `diags` offset parameter: for array offset o, it has i, j with i - j = -o? Let's check: `diags(diagonals, offsets, shape)`: offset k indicates which sub/super diagonal: main k=0, k>0 above? Let's develop `lp_solve_one(a)` robust:
```
def lp_solve_one(a):
    n=len(a); if n>LP_MAX: return None
    a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<1e-12 return None
    a=a/s
    if a.max()==0: return None
    M = convolve_fft(a).max()
    if M<=0: return None
    # floor small
    a_mat = np.maximum(a, 1e-12); a_mat/=a_mat.sum()
    # build sparse matrix
    try:
       cols = np.repeat(np.arange(n), n, dtype=np.int32)
       rows = cols + np.tile(np.arange(n), n, dtype=np.int32)
       data = np.tile(a_mat, n).astype(np.float64)
       A = csr_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape=(2*n-1,n), dtype=np.float64)
       # If all a positive, full. If using floor full.
       c_obj = -np.ones(n)
       b_ub = np.full(2*n-1, M)
       bounds = [(0.0, 1e6)]*n  # list of tuples large memory? For n1024 okay.
       res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
       if res.success and res.x is not None:
          b=np.asarray(res.x)
          b=np.maximum(b,0)
          if b.sum()>0:
             return b/b.sum()
    except Exception:
       pass
    return None
```
Potential memory: `bounds = [(0.0, 1e6)] * n` creates list n, okay. But `linprog` may convert to arrays.

`lp_improve` mixes:
```
def lp_improve(a, deadline, max_lps):
   a=normalize(a); best=a.copy(); best_score=score(a)
   current=a.copy()
   for _ in range(max_lps):
      if time>deadline: break
      b=lp_solve_one(current)
      if b is None: break
      improved=False
      for t in [1.0,0.8,0.6,0.4,0.2]:
          cand=normalize((1-t)*current + t*b)
          sc=score(cand)
          if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
              best_score=sc; best=cand; current=cand; improved=True
          elif sc < current_score... maybe
      if not improved:
          # try current = b if its score < current_score?
          bsc=score(b)
          if bsc < score(current): current=b; else break
      # else continue
   return best,best_score
```

Resampling function:
```
def resample_sequence(seq, n):
    x=np.asarray(seq,float); x=np.maximum(x,0); if len==0 or sum0 return ones(n)/n
    old=np.arange(len(x)); new=np.linspace(0, len(x)-1, n)
    y=np.interp(new, old, x)
    return normalize(y)
```
For anti-pal? not needed.

Sine init:
```
def sine_init(n, p, alpha=1.0):
   i=np.arange(n)
   x=np.sin(2*np.pi*p*(i+0.5)/n)
   a=1.0 + alpha*x
   a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   return normalize(a)
```
Use p=1,2,3, maybe 4. α=1 gives nonnegative min0. Actually sin min -1, a min0. Good.

Random smooth init maybe:
```
def random_smooth_anti(n, rng, max_freq=8):
   # random Fourier sine coefficients for first max_freq anti harmonics
   coeffs = rng.normal(0,1, (max_freq,)) / np.sqrt(n)
   i=np.arange(n)
   x = np.zeros(n)
   for p,c in enumerate(coeffs,1): x += c*np.sin(2*np.pi*p*(i+0.5)/n)
   x = np.tanh(x/ max(1e-6,np.abs(x).max()))
   a=1+x; normalize
```
This gives smooth anti-pal. Good.

Spectral init maybe less necessary if random smooth.

```
def normalize_simplex(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a[np.isnan(a)]=0? Better if not finite return uniform.
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return np.ones(a.size)/a.size
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s < 1e-12:
       return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)/a.size
    a /= s
    return a
```
Since sum1 values ≤1.

`simplex_project(v)` as above. Need if n=0? Not. If sizes large, use float64.
```
def simplex_project(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64)
    n=v.size
    if n==0: return v
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    sv=np.cumsum(u)
    idx=np.arange(1,n+1)
    cond = u*idx > (sv - 1.0)
    if not np.any(cond):
        return np.ones(n)/n
    rho=int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][0])
    theta=(sv[rho]-1.0)/(rho+1.0)
    out=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    s=out.sum()
    if s <=0: return np.ones(n)/n
    out /= s
    return out
```
This projection returns sum1. It assumes target sum=1. Good.

`convolution_fft(a)`:
```
def convolution_fft(a):
   n=a.size; M=next_pow2(2*n-1); fa=np.fft.rfft(a,M); c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,M)[:2*n-1]; return np.clip(c,0,None)
```
For score, use this.

`compute_c_h(a, K)` with M=next_pow2(3*n+1). It uses `fa` length M; conv c from fa*fa with M sufficient (M>=2n-1). corr h length n from fa*fw.conj. Need `fw = np.fft.rfft(w, M)`. Good.
```
def compute_c_h(a,K):
   n=a.size; L=2*n-1; M=next_pow2(3*n+1)
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,M)[:L]
   c=np.clip(c,0,None)
   m=float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
   if m <= 1e-300: return c,m,np.zeros(n)
   if K <= 0:
      w=np.full(L, 1.0/L)
   else:
      z=(c-m)/(m*float(K))
      # z max 0, min maybe -inf? if c=0 and m small, z=-1/K finite.
      w=np.exp(z)
      s=w.sum()
      if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
          w=np.full(L,1.0/L)
      else:
          w /= s
   fw=np.fft.rfft(w,M)
   h=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw.conj(),M)[:n]
   h=np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
   return c,m,h
```
Note z=(c-m)/(m*K) = (c/m -1)/K. If K=1, z in [-1,0]. For K=0 uniform. Good.

```
   a=np.clip(a,0,None); s=a.sum(); if s<0.01: return inf
   a=a/s; c=convolution_fft(a); return 2*a.size*c.max()
```

Adam function:
```
def adam_optimize(a0, deadline, k_values, total_iters, rng, label=''):
    a=normalize_simplex(a0); n=a.size
    if n < 2: return a, fast_score_sequence(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=2*n*convolution_fft(a).max()
    phases=len(k_values)
    # allocate more iters to later high K? Use weights [1,1,1,1,2]
    weights=[1,1,1,1,2]... normalize sum total_iters
    for idx,K in enumerate(k_values):
       if time.monotonic()>=deadline: break
       it=max(50, int(total_iters*weights[idx]/sum(weights)))
       cur=best.copy(); mom=np.zeros(n); momv=np.zeros(n)
       lr0=0.03/(1.0+0.4*np.log1p(float(K)))
       beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; eps=1e-8
       no_improve=0; stag_limit=300
       for it_i in range(it):
          if time.monotonic()>=deadline: break
          c,m,h=compute_c_h(cur,K)
          sc=2*n*m
          if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
             best_sc=sc; best=cur.copy(); no_improve=0
          else:
             no_improve += 1
          if no_improve > stag_limit:
             cur=perturb_mixture(best, rng, 0.25, 0.1)
             mom.fill(0); momv.fill(0); no_improve=0
          dmax=np.max(np.abs(h)) if h.size else 0
          if dmax <= 1e-300: break
          # Adam on normalized h? We can normalize h to max 1 to control
          h_norm = h / dmax
          mom = beta1*mom + (1-beta1)*h_norm
          momv = beta2*momv + (1-beta2)*(h_norm*h_norm)
          step = mom / (np.sqrt(momv) + eps)
          cur = simplex_project(cur - lr0*step)
       # end phase
       # maybe evaluate best
    return best,best_sc
```
We normalize h by dmax, so step magnitude ~1. lr0 0.03 gives coordinate change up to 0.03. Projection. Good. For n=1000, a ~0.001; 0.03 is 30 times typical, but only where sign extreme and clipping; projection may shift. Maybe too high. Use lr0=0.01. Let's set base 0.01. For K high, 0.003.
`lr0 = 0.01 / (1 + 0.3*log1p(K))`. K1 0.005, K32 0.0015. Safe.

If using normalized h, gradient direction independent of scale. Step 0.005 coordinate absolute. Since a values 1/n ~0.001, step equivalent 5* a; okay. For n small (128), a~0.008, step abs 0.005 less. Good.

Could adapt lr to 1/n: `lr0 = 5.0 / n / (1+...)`? If a~1/n, step 5/n, similar. Use `lr0 = min(0.02, 10.0/n) / (1+0.3*log1p(K))`. For n=1000, 0.01; n=128,0.02; n=4096,0.0024. Good. Let's use this: base = min(0.05, 20.0/n). Then divide. `lr = base / (1 + 0.35*log1p(K))`.

Perturb function:
```
def perturb_mixture(a, rng, mult_sigma, mix_t):
    n=a.size
    r = rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n)
    anti = r - r[::-1]
    a2 = a + mult_sigma * a * anti
    if mix_t > 0:
       b = rng.random(n)
       a2 = (1.0 - mix_t)*a2 + mix_t*b
    return normalize_simplex(a2)
```
If a has zeros, anti noise doesn't add to zeros; mixing random adds.

Spectral init function maybe too heavy; but generate once per length. Implement without too many fetches. Need `cap_convolution_target`:
```
def cap_convolution_target(c, M):
   c=np.asarray(c,float); L=c.size
   c_total = c.sum()
   if c_total <=0: return c + 1.0/L
   if M <=0: M = c.max()/2
   clipped = np.minimum(c, M)
   sum_clip = clipped.sum()
   if sum_clip >= c_total - 1e-12:
       return clipped / sum_clip  # normalize to total
   unc = clipped < M
   headroom = (M - clipped[unc]).sum() if np.any(unc) else 0
   if headroom <= 1e-12:
       return clipped / sum_clip
   needed = c_total - sum_clip
   add_frac = min(1.0, needed / headroom)
   out = clipped.copy()
   out[unc] += add_frac * (M - out[unc])
   # maybe due round sum not exactly total; normalize to total
   s=out.sum()
   if s>0: out = out * (c_total / s)
   return out
```
If add_frac<1, sum exactly total without exceeding M. Good.

Spectral sqrt:
```
def spectral_bake(a, target_ratio, iters):
    n=a.size; a=normalize(a)
    best=a; best_sc=...
    for _ in range(iters):
       c=convolution_fft(a); m=c.max()
       Mtarget = min(target_ratio / n, 0.95*m)
       if Mtarget <= m: maybe no change
       c_t=cap_convolution_target(c, Mtarget)
       L=c_t.size; M=next_pow2(L)
       C=np.fft.rfft(c_t, M)
       A=np.sqrt(C)
       # enforce real by irfft
       cand1=np.fft.irfft(A,M)[:n]
       cand2=-cand1
       for cand in (cand1,cand2):
          y=np.clip(cand,0,None)
          y=normalize(y)
          sc=2*n*convolution_fft(y).max()
          if sc<best_sc: best=y; best_sc=sc
          # maybe abs
          y=np.abs(cand); y=normalize(y); sc=...
       # blend
       a=normalize(0.7*best + 0.3*a)
    return best,best_sc
```
This may produce same. Use it for 1-2 inits.

Family function:
```
def harmonic_sequence(coeffs, n, P):
    i=np.arange(n)
    X=np.zeros(n)
    for p,c in enumerate(coeffs,1):
       X += c*np.sin(2*np.pi*p*(i+0.5)/n)

    return normalize(a)
```

```
def run_harmonic_search(n, P, deadline, rng):
   best_coeffs = np.zeros(P); best_sc=...; best_seq=...
   inits=[]

      for amp in [0.5,1.0,1.5,-0.5,-1.0,-1.5]:

      except: ...
```

`normalize_simplex(a)`: If a is list, convert. If size n=0? Return empty? But propose needs nonempty. In helpers, if n=0 return np.array([1.0]). Implement:
```
def normalize_simplex(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0:
       return np.array([1.0])
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
       return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)/a.size
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    a=a.copy()
    s=a.sum()
    if s < 1e-12:
       return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)/a.size
    return a/s
```
If a multidim? Flatten? Use `a.ravel()`. Good.

`anti_random_init`: Use tanh normal. For n odd, middle 0.
```
def anti_random_init(n,rng):
   x=np.zeros(n)
   m=n//2
   z=rng.normal(0,1,m)
   x[:m]=z
   if n%2==0:
      x[m:] = -z[::-1]
   else:
      x[m+1:] = -z[::-1]
   x=np.tanh(x)
   return normalize(1+x)
```


`lp_solve_one(a)`:
Need import csr_matrix. For n > LP_MAX, return None.
```
def lp_solve_one(a):
    global LP_MAX? Use constant.
    n=a.size
    if n < 2 or n > LP_MAX: return None
    a=normalize_simplex(a)
    c=convolution_fft(a); M=float(c.max())
    if M <= 1e-15: return None
    a_mat=np.maximum(a,1e-12); a_mat/=a_mat.sum()
    try:
       cols=np.repeat(np.arange(n,dtype=np.int32), n)
       rows=cols + np.tile(np.arange(n,dtype=np.int32), n)
       data=np.tile(a_mat / M, n).astype(np.float64)
       A=csr_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape=(2*n-1,n), dtype=np.float64)
       b=np.ones(2*n-1,dtype=np.float64)
       obj=-np.ones(n,dtype=np.float64)
       res=linprog(obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, np.inf), method='highs')
       if res.success and res.x is not None:
           b=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64)
           b=np.maximum(b,0)
           s=b.sum()
           if s > 1.0 + 1e-7:
               return b/s
           return None
    except Exception:
       pass
    return None
```
Why require s>1? For mix maybe if s≈1 no improvement. Return None. But if s=1 and b different could mix improve? Unlikely.

Could `linprog` with `bounds=(0.0, np.inf)` fail? It should. If not, maybe use list. We can catch and retry dense with bounds list? Not.

Potential scale: If M very small, a_mat/M huge, constraints restrict; z could overflow? a_mat<=1, M~1/n, ratio~n; for n=1536 1536, fine.

```
def lp_improve(a0, stop_time, max_lps):
   a=normalize(a0); best=a.copy(); best_sc=fast_score(a)
   current=a.copy(); current_sc=best_sc
   for outer in range(max_lps):
      if time>=stop_time: break
      b=lp_solve_one(current)
      if b is None: break
      # evaluate b and mixes
      sc_b=fast_score(b)
      candidates=[]
      for t in [1.0,0.8,0.6,0.4,0.2]:
          cand=normalize((1.0-t)*current + t*b)
          sc=fast_score(cand)
          if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
              best=cand; best_sc=sc; current=cand; current_sc=sc
           # track best among candidates even if not below best? maybe for next
      # Need if current not improved but b has lower current_sc, set current to best candidate (maybe mix) to continue
      # Re-evaluate best candidate? We can in loop keep `local_best_cand/sc`
```

```python
"""
Hybrid search for low-peak nonnegative step functions.

The objective is scale invariant, so sequences are searched on the simplex
(sum = 1).  The search combines:

1. Low-dimensional harmonic anti-palindromic seeds.
2. Active LP-style local improvements of the form max sum(b)
   subject to a*b <= max(a*a).
3. Smooth log-sum-conv + Adam simplex projected refinement.
4. A few deterministic fallbacks, including the provided construction if present.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize

LP_MAX = 1024


def next_pow2(n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    return 1 << (n - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_simplex(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def simplex_project(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    sv = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = u * idx > (sv - 1.0)
    if not np.any(cond):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    rho = int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][0])
    theta = (sv[rho] - 1.0) / float(rho + 1)
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return out / s


def convolution_fft(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    M = next_pow2(L)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, M)[:L]
    return np.clip(c, 0.0, None)


def fast_score_sequence(seq):
    a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return float("inf")
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    a = a / s
    c = convolution_fft(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * float(c.max()))


def direct_score_sequence(seq):
    a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return float("inf")
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * float(np.max(c)) / (s * s))


def resample_sequence(seq, n):
    x = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, None)
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if x.size == 0 or x.sum() <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if x.size == 1:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    old = np.arange(x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, x.size - 1, n, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.interp(new, old, x)
    return normalize_simplex(y)


def sine_init(n, p, alpha=1.0):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(p) * (i + 0.5) / float(n))
    a = 1.0 + float(alpha) * np.tanh(x)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    return normalize_simplex(a)


def anti_random_init(n, rng):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    m = n // 2
    x = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if m > 0:
        z = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, m)
        x[:m] = z
        if n % 2 == 0:
            x[m:] = -z[::-1]
        else:
            x[m + 1:] = -z[::-1]
    x = np.tanh(x)
    return normalize_simplex(1.0 + x)


def random_smooth_anti(n, rng, max_freq=5):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    max_freq = int(min(max(1, max_freq), 12, n // 2))
    coeffs = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, max_freq)
    coeffs /= (1.0 + rng.uniform(0.5, 2.0))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    X = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for p in range(max_freq):
        c = coeffs[p]
        if abs(c) > 1e-12:
            X += c * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(p + 1) * (i + 0.5) / float(n))
    a = 1.0 + np.tanh(X)
    return normalize_simplex(a)


def harmonic_sequence(coeffs, n, P):
    coeffs = np.asarray(coeffs, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if coeffs.size < P:
        tmp = np.zeros(P, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(P, coeffs.size)
        tmp[:m] = coeffs[:m]
        coeffs = tmp
    else:
        coeffs = coeffs[:P].copy()
    coeffs = np.where(np.isfinite(coeffs), coeffs, 0.0)

    n = int(max(1, n))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    X = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for p in range(P):
        c = coeffs[p]
        if abs(c) > 1e-12:
            X += c * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(p + 1) * (i + 0.5) / float(n))
    a = 1.0 + np.tanh(X)
    return normalize_simplex(a)


def harmonic_objective(x, n, P):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e10
    a = harmonic_sequence(x, n, P)
    sc = fast_score_sequence(a)
    return sc if np.isfinite(sc) else 1e10


def run_harmonic_search(n, P, deadline, rng):
    n = int(max(8, n))
    P = int(max(1, min(8, P)))
    coeffs0 = np.zeros(P, dtype=np.float64)
    best_seq = harmonic_sequence(coeffs0, n, P)
    best_sc = fast_score_sequence(best_seq)

    inits = [coeffs0.copy()]
    for p in range(min(P, 4)):
        for amp in (0.7, 1.2, -0.7, -1.2):
            c = np.zeros(P, dtype=np.float64)
            c[p] = amp
            inits.append(c)
    inits.append(np.zeros(P, dtype=np.float64))
    for _ in range(5):
        inits.append(rng.uniform(-1.5, 1.5, P).astype(np.float64))

    bounds = [(-2.0, 2.0) for _ in range(P)]
    opts = {"maxiter": 80, "ftol": 1e-10, "gtol": 1e-5}

    for x0 in inits:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(harmonic_objective, x0, args=(n, P),
                           method="L-BFGS-B", bounds=bounds, options=opts)
            cand_x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel() if res.x is not None else x0
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(cand_x)):
                cand_x = x0
            cand_seq = harmonic_sequence(cand_x, n, P)
            sc = fast_score_sequence(cand_seq)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_seq = cand_seq
        except Exception:
            pass

    sc = fast_score_sequence(best_seq)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        best_sc = 2.0
        best_seq = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        best_sc = sc
    return best_seq, best_sc


def update_promising(promising, seq, score):
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if seq.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(seq)):
        return
    if len(promising) < 30:
        promising.append((float(score), seq.copy()))
        return
    worst_idx = 0
    worst_score = promising[0][0]
    for i in range(1, len(promising)):
        if promising[i][0] > worst_score:
            worst_score = promising[i][0]
            worst_idx = i
    if float(score) < worst_score:
        promising[worst_idx] = (float(score), seq.copy())


def compute_c_h(a, K):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    M = next_pow2(3 * n + 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, M)[:L]
    c = np.clip(c, 0.0, None)
    m = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if m <= 1e-300:
        return c, m, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if K is None or float(K) <= 0.0:
        w = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        z = (c - m) * (float(K) / m)
        w = np.exp(z)
        s = float(w.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            w = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            w = w / s

    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, M)
    h = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw.conj(), M)[:n]
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return c, m, h


def perturb_mixture(a, rng, mult_sigma=0.25, mix_t=0.05):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return a
    r = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
    anti = r - r[::-1]
    a2 = a + float(mult_sigma) * a * anti
    if mix_t > 0.0:
        b = rng.random(n)
        a2 = (1.0 - float(mix_t)) * a2 + float(mix_t) * b
    return normalize_simplex(a2)


def adam_optimize(a0, stop_time, k_values, max_iters, rng):
    a0 = normalize_simplex(a0)
    n = a0.size
    if n < 2 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a0)):
        return a0, fast_score_sequence(a0)

    best = a0.copy()
    c0 = convolution_fft(best)
    best_sc = float(2.0 * n * c0.max()) if c0.size else 2.0

    weights = [1.0 for _ in range(len(k_values))]
    if len(weights) >= 2:
        weights[-1] = 2.0
    if len(weights) >= 3:
        weights[-2] = 2.0

    for idx, K in enumerate(k_values):
        now = time.monotonic()
        remaining = float(stop_time) - now
        if remaining <= 0.0:
            break
        remw = float(sum(weights[idx:] or [1.0]))
        phase_stop = min(float(stop_time), now + remaining * (float(weights[idx]) / remw))

        cur = best.copy()
        mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        momv = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        beta1, beta2 = 0.90, 0.999
        eps = 1e-8
        base_lr = min(0.05, 20.0 / float(n))
        lr = base_lr / (1.0 + 0.35 * float(np.log1p(max(0.1, float(K)))))
        no_improve = 0
        stag_limit = 250

        for _ in range(int(max_iters)):
            now2 = time.monotonic()
            if now2 >= phase_stop:
                break
            c, m, h = compute_c_h(cur, K)
            sc = float(2.0 * n * m)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = sc
                best = cur.copy()
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1

            if no_improve > stag_limit:
                cur = perturb_mixture(best, rng, 0.25, 0.08)
                mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                momv = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                no_improve = 0

            dmax = float(np.max(np.abs(h))) if h.size else 0.0
            if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 1e-300:
                break

            hn = h / dmax
            mom = beta1 * mom + (1.0 - beta1) * hn
            momv = beta2 * momv + (1.0 - beta2) * (hn * hn)
            step = mom / (np.sqrt(momv) + eps)
            cur = simplex_project(cur - lr * step)

    c_best = convolution_fft(best)
    best_sc = float(2.0 * n * c_best.max()) if c_best.size else best_sc
    return best, best_sc


def lp_solve_one(a):
    a = normalize_simplex(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 4 or n > LP_MAX:
        return None

    c = convolution_fft(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return None
    M = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 1e-15:
        return None

    a_mat = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    s = float(a_mat.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return None
    a_mat = a_mat / s
    scale = a_mat / M

    try:
        base = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32)
        cols = np.repeat(base, n)
        rows = cols + np.tile(base, n)
        data = np.tile(scale, n).astype(np.float64)
        A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
        A[rows, cols] = data

        obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        b_ub = np.ones(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        res = linprog(obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=(0.0, np.inf), method="highs")
        if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            b = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
            b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
            bs = float(b.sum())
            if bs > 1.0 + 1e-7:
                return b / bs
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def lp_improve(a0, stop_time, max_lps):
    a0 = normalize_simplex(a0)
    current = a0.copy()
    current_sc = fast_score_sequence(current)
    best = a0.copy()
    best_sc = current_sc

    for _ in range(int(max(0, max_lps))):
        if time.monotonic() > stop_time:
            break
        b = lp_solve_one(current)
        if b is None:
            break

        local_best = None
        local_best_sc = float("inf")
        for t in (1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.4, 0.2):
            cand = normalize_simplex((1.0 - t) * current + t * b)
            sc = fast_score_sequence(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_best_sc:
                local_best_sc = sc
                local_best = cand

        if local_best is None:
            break

        if local_best_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best = local_best.copy()
            best_sc = local_best_sc

        if local_best_sc < current_sc - 1e-12:
            current = local_best.copy()
            current_sc = local_best_sc
        else:
            break

    return best, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    fallback = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) / 256.0
    try:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
        start = time.monotonic()
        budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
        final_deadline = start + budget - 6.0

        best_seq = fallback.copy()
        best_score = fast_score_sequence(best_seq)
        promising = []
        update_promising(promising, best_seq, best_score)

        # Explicit small construction: [1, 1, 1, 0, 1], objective 1.875.
        explicit = normalize_simplex(np.array([1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.0, 1.0]))
        explicit_sc = fast_score_sequence(explicit)
        if explicit_sc < best_score:
            best_score = explicit_sc
            best_seq = explicit
        update_promising(promising, best_seq, best_score)

        # Use the previously supplied construction if available.
        try:
            hseq = height_sequence_1
            hnorm = normalize_simplex(hseq)
            hnorm = hnorm[~np.isnan(hnorm)] if hnorm.size else hnorm
            hsc = fast_score_sequence(hnorm)
            if np.isfinite(hsc) and hsc < best_score:
                best_score = hsc
                best_seq = hnorm.copy()
            update_promising(promising, hnorm, hsc)
            have_height = True
        except Exception:
            have_height = False

        print("initial best:", best_score, "len:", best_seq.size, flush=True)

        harm_stop = min(final_deadline, start + min(420.0, 0.22 * budget))
        harm_specs = [(256, 4), (512, 5), (1024, 5), (2048, 4)]
        for n, P in harm_specs:
            if time.monotonic() > harm_stop:
                break
            try:
                seq, sc = run_harmonic_search(n, P, harm_stop, rng)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_seq = seq.copy()
                    print("harmonic n=%d score=%.6f" % (n, sc), flush=True)
                update_promising(promising, seq, sc)
            except Exception:
                pass

        lp_stop = min(final_deadline, start + min(1050.0, 0.52 * budget))

        lp_queue = []
        # Promising sequences first.
        prom_copy = list(promising)
        # select up to 12 by score without lambda
        selected = []
        used = [False] * len(prom_copy)
        for _ in range(min(12, len(prom_copy))):
            bi = -1
            bv = float("inf")
            for i in range(len(prom_copy)):
                if used[i] and prom_copy[i][0] < bv:
                    bv = prom_copy[i][0]
                    bi = i
            if bi < 0:
                break
            used[bi] = True
            selected.append(prom_copy[bi][1])
        for seq in selected:
            if seq.size <= LP_MAX:
                lp_queue.append(seq.copy())

        # Structured LP seeds.
        for n in (128, 256, 512, 1024):
            if n > LP_MAX:
                continue
            for p in (1, 2, 3, 4):
                lp_queue.append(sine_init(n, p, 1.0))
            lp_queue.append(random_smooth_anti(n, rng, 5))
            lp_queue.append(anti_random_init(n, rng))
            if have_height:
                lp_queue.append(resample_sequence(height_sequence_1, n))

        processed = 0
        for seq in lp_queue:
            if time.monotonic() > lp_stop:
                break
            seq = normalize_simplex(seq)
            if seq.size < 8 or seq.size > LP_MAX:
                continue
            if processed % 4 == 0 and time.monotonic() > lp_stop:
                break
            per_budget = 70.0 if seq.size <= 512 else 120.0
            sub_stop = min(lp_stop, time.monotonic() + per_budget)
            try:
                res, sc = lp_improve(seq, sub_stop, max_lps=3 if seq.size <= 512 else 2)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_seq = res.copy()
                    print("lp n=%d score=%.6f" % (seq.size, sc), flush=True)
                update_promising(promising, res, sc)
                processed += 1
            except Exception:
                pass

        adam_stop = max(harm_stop, min(final_deadline, start + budget - 180.0))

        adam_candidates = []
        adam_candidates.append(best_seq.copy())
        prom_copy = list(promising)
        used = [False] * len(prom_copy)
        for _ in range(min(10, len(prom_copy))):
            bi = -1
            bv = float("inf")
            for i in range(len(prom_copy)):
                if used[i] and prom_copy[i][0] < bv:
                    bv = prom_copy[i][0]
                    bi = i
            if bi < 0:
                break
            used[bi] = True
            s0 = prom_copy[bi][1]
            if s0.size <= 4096:
                adam_candidates.append(s0.copy())

        for n in (256, 512, 1024, 2048):
            adam_candidates.append(sine_init(n, 2, 1.0))
            adam_candidates.append(sine_init(n, 3, 1.0))
            adam_candidates.append(random_smooth_anti(n, rng, 6))
            if have_height:
                r = resample_sequence(height_sequence_1, n)
                if r.size == n:
                    adam_candidates.append(r)

        # De-duplicate roughly by length and score order already in list.
        processed = 0
        for seq in adam_candidates:
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now > adam_stop - 40.0:
                break
            seq = normalize_simplex(seq)
            n = seq.size
            if n < 8 or n > 4096:
                continue
            base_budget = 50.0 if n <= 256 else 80.0 if n <= 1024 else 140.0
            sub_stop = min(adam_stop, now + base_budget)
            try:
                res, sc = adam_optimize(seq, sub_stop, [1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0], 1000000, rng)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_seq = res.copy()
                    print("adam n=%d score=%.6f" % (n, sc), flush=True)
                update_promising(promising, res, sc)
                processed += 1
            except Exception:
                pass

        # Final LP polish on the current champion if it is short enough and time remains.
        polish_stop = final_deadline
        if best_seq.size <= LP_MAX and time.monotonic() < polish_stop - 90.0:
            try:
                sub_stop = min(polish_stop, time.monotonic() + 240.0)
                res, sc = lp_improve(best_seq, sub_stop, 3)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_seq = res.copy()
                    print("final polish lp score=%.6f" % sc, flush=True)
                update_promising(promising, res, sc)
            except Exception:
                pass

        # Verify a small set of finalists with direct convolution / evaluator.
        finalists = [best_seq]
        prom_copy = list(promising)
        used = [False] * len(prom_copy)
        for _ in range(min(10, len(prom_copy))):
            bi = -1
            bv = float("inf")
            for i in range(len(prom_copy)):
                if used[i] and prom_copy[i][0] < bv:
                    bv = prom_copy[i][0]
                    bi = i
            if bi < 0:
                break
            used[bi] = True
            finalists.append(prom_copy[bi][1])

        final_seq = best_seq.copy()
        final_score = fast_score_sequence(final_seq)

        for cand in finalists:
            cand = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if cand.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(cand)):
                continue
            # Only direct-check reasonably sized sequences; evaluator can handle bigger,
            # but avoid paying for very long direct convolution unnecessarily here.
            try:
                if cand.size <= 6000:
                    sc = direct_score_sequence(cand.tolist())
                else:
                    sc = fast_score_sequence(cand)
            except Exception:
                sc = fast_score_sequence(cand)

            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_score:
                final_score = sc
                final_seq = cand.copy()

        # Prefer the sequence whose judge score is lower, if the judge function is available.
        try:
            judge = evaluate_sequence
        except Exception:
            judge = None

        if judge is not None:
            verified_seq = final_seq.copy()
            verified_score = float("inf")
            try:
                judge_score = float(judge(verified_seq.tolist()))
                if np.isfinite(judge_score):
                    verified_score = judge_score
            except Exception:
                pass

            for cand in finalists[:12]:
                cand = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                if cand.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(cand)):
                    continue
                try:
                    sc = float(judge(cand.tolist()))
                except Exception:
                    sc = float("inf")
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < verified_score:
                    verified_score = sc
                    verified_seq = cand.copy()

            verified_seq = np.clip(verified_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if verified_seq.size and np.all(np.isfinite(verified_seq)) and verified_seq.sum() >= 0.01:
                final_seq = verified_seq
                final_score = verified_score

        # Last sanity fallback.
        final_seq = np.asarray(final_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if final_seq.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(final_seq)) or final_seq.sum() < 0.01:
            final_seq = fallback.copy()

        out = [float(x) for x in np.clip(final_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)]
        print("final proposed score estimate:", final_score, "len:", len(out), flush=True)
        return out

    except Exception:
        return [float(x) for x in fallback]
```
